3300// AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
3301// TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-737): Review public DBT dispatch and child environment ownership.
3302/// Dispatch a command onto the Detcore-linked reverie-dbt runtime.
3303#[cfg(feature = "dbt")]
3304async fn run_dbt(
3305    command: Command,
3306    config: DetConfig,
3307    print_summary: bool,
3308    capture_output: bool,
3309) -> Result<Output, Error> {
3310    if !config.sequentialize_threads {
3311        return Err(anyhow!(
3312            "the dbt backend requires sequentialized threads; remove \
3313             --no-sequentialize-threads (or --strace-only) to run under --backend dbt"
3314        ));
3315    }
3316
3317    // The guest sees this string in its environment, so it keeps the encoding
3318    // it had before the backend facts moved into `Config::backend` and before
3319    // three constant switches were deleted; see `dbt_detconfig_json`. The DBT
3320    // runtime decodes the backend facts back out of it (here DBT's own); when
3321    // the string is absent or does not parse, it uses the strict default's
3322    // facts, which are ptrace's.
3323    let config_json = dbt_detconfig_json(&config)
3324        .map_err(|error| anyhow!("failed to serialize the Detcore config for DBT: {error}"))?;
3325    let panic_on_unsupported_syscalls = config.panic_on_unsupported_syscalls;
3326    let (drrun, client) = detcore_dbt::prepare_native_client()
3327        .map_err(|error| anyhow!("failed to prepare the Detcore DynamoRIO client: {error}"))?;
3328    let mut runner = reverie_dbt::DbtRunner::new(&drrun, &client)
3329        .map_err(|error| {
3330            anyhow!(
3331                "failed to configure the DynamoRIO DBT runner (drrun={}, client={}): {error}",
3332                drrun.display(),
3333                client.display()
3334            )
3335        })?
3336        .summary(print_summary)
3337        .isolated_process_group(panic_on_unsupported_syscalls);
3338    if panic_on_unsupported_syscalls {
3339        runner = runner.client_argument("-panic-on-unsupported-syscalls");
3340    }
3341
3342    let program = command.get_program().to_owned();
3343    let mut environment = command.get_captured_envs();
3344    environment.insert(detcore_dbt::DETCONFIG_ENV.into(), config_json.into());
3345    let guest = command.into_std_lossy();
3346    tracing::info!(
3347        target: "hermit::dbt",
3348        program = ?program,
3349        drrun = %drrun.display(),
3350        client = %client.display(),
3351        "launching guest through reverie-dbt with Detcore<DbtGuest>",
3352    );
3353
3354    let (status, stdout, stderr, mut global) = if capture_output {
3355        let launch = || {
3356            runner.output_with_environment_and_global::<detcore::GlobalState>(
3357                &guest,
3358                &environment,
3359                config.clone(),
3360            )
3361        };
3362        let (mut output, mut global) = launch()
3363            .await
3364            .map_err(|error| anyhow!("failed to launch drrun ({}): {error}", drrun.display()))?;
3365        if dbt_client_thread_start_failed(&output.status) {
3366            tracing::warn!(
3367                target: "hermit::dbt",
3368                "DynamoRIO client thread failed before guest start; retrying once",
3369            );
3370            global.force_shutdown_with_error();
3371            global.cancel_internal_scheduler().await;
3372            global.clean_up(false, &None).await;
3373            (output, global) = launch().await.map_err(|error| {
3374                anyhow!("failed to launch drrun ({}): {error}", drrun.display())
3375            })?;
3376        }
3377        (output.status, output.stdout, output.stderr, global)
3378    } else {
3379        let launch = || {
3380            runner.status_with_environment_and_global::<detcore::GlobalState>(
3381                &guest,
3382                &environment,
3383                config.clone(),
3384            )
3385        };
3386        let (mut status, mut global) = launch()
3387            .await
3388            .map_err(|error| anyhow!("failed to launch drrun ({}): {error}", drrun.display()))?;
3389        if dbt_client_thread_start_failed(&status) {
3390            tracing::warn!(
3391                target: "hermit::dbt",
3392                "DynamoRIO client thread failed before guest start; retrying once",
3393            );
3394            global.force_shutdown_with_error();
3395            global.cancel_internal_scheduler().await;
3396            global.clean_up(false, &None).await;
3397            (status, global) = launch().await.map_err(|error| {
3398                anyhow!("failed to launch drrun ({}): {error}", drrun.display())
3399            })?;
3400        }
3401        (status, Vec::new(), Vec::new(), global)
3402    };
3403
3404    if !status.success() {
3405        global.force_shutdown_with_error();
3406        global.cancel_internal_scheduler().await;
3407    }
3408    global.clean_up(print_summary, &None).await;
3409    Ok(Output {
3410        status: status.into(),
3411        stdout,
3412        stderr,
3413    })
3414}
3415
3416// NOTE: A single-threaded executor is used here so that the tokio threads
3417// themselves wouldn't contribute non-determinism to the PID namespace. This
3418// could also be changed to a specific number of threads and that would be
3419// deterministic, but it shouldn't be based on the number of cores. When the
3420// thread count is based off of the number of cores in the machine, then two
3421// runs on different machines with a different number of cores will not be the
3422// same.
3423/// Run the given command as deterministically as possible.
3424pub fn run(
3425    command: Command,
3426    config: DetConfig,
3427    print_summary: bool,
3428    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3429) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
3430    run_with_backend(
3431        command,
3432        config,
3433        print_summary,
3434        print_summary_to_json_file,
3435        Backend::Ptrace,
3436    )
3437}
3438
3439/// Run the given command using the selected instrumentation backend.
3440pub fn run_with_backend(
3441    command: Command,
3442    config: DetConfig,
3443    print_summary: bool,
3444    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3445    backend: Backend,
3446) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
3447    run_with_backend_timeout(
3448        command,
3449        config,
3450        print_summary,
3451        print_summary_to_json_file,
3452        backend,
3453        None,
3454    )
3455}
3456
3457/// [`run_with_backend`] with a hermit-enforced wall-clock bound on the guest.
3458///
3459/// This library never installs a process alarm. Ordinary ptrace keeps the
3460/// original runtime, operation, failed GlobalState and backing guards when a
3461/// finite cleanup attempt is unconfirmed; downcast [`HermitCleanupUnconfirmed`]
3462/// and recover it on the original process/thread. A timeout is measured before
3463/// startup and is not a certificate of cleanup. `None` leaves execution
3464/// unbounded, but fatal cleanup attempts remain finite. Nonordinary backends
3465/// retain their existing cooperative timeout/drop semantics. Synchronous stalls
3466/// require caller-owned supervision; only the CLI owns its hard alarm fallback.
3467///
3468/// ⚠️ NOT PART OF `DetConfig`, DELIBERATELY. A wall-clock bound is host state,
3469/// and `DetConfig` is the determinism configuration that is serialized to disk
3470/// and reasoned about as guest-visible. Threading a real-time deadline through
3471/// it would put a nondeterministic quantity inside the structure whose entire
3472/// job is to exclude them.
3473pub fn run_with_backend_timeout(
3474    command: Command,
3475    config: DetConfig,
3476    print_summary: bool,
3477    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3478    backend: Backend,
3479    timeout: Option<Duration>,
3480) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
3481    if backend == Backend::Ptrace {
3482        let summary_path = print_summary_to_json_file.clone();
3483        return ptrace_completion::run(timeout, move |control| async move {
3484            let report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
3485            let config = prepare_backend_config(config, backend);
3486            let result = dispatch_backend(
3487                command,
3488                config,
3489                print_summary,
3490                &summary_path,
3491                backend,
3492                Some(control),
3493            )
3494            .await;
3495            report.finish(result)
3496        });
3497    }
3498    let skid_overshoot_report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(backend.uses_ptrace_pmu_timers());
3499    if backend == Backend::Kvm {
3500        ensure_kvm_stdin_reserved()?;
3501    }
3502    let config = prepare_backend_config(config, backend);
3503    let result = run_with_backend_inner(
3504        command,
3505        config,
3506        print_summary,
3507        print_summary_to_json_file,
3508        backend,
3509        timeout,
3510    );
3511    skid_overshoot_report.finish(result)
3512}
3513
3514// TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-749): Review LiteInst backend configuration normalization.
3515#[doc(hidden)]
3516pub fn prepare_backend_config(mut config: DetConfig, backend: Backend) -> DetConfig {
3517    // Detcore reads what the selected backend can do, never which backend it is.
3518    // Each backend states its own facts in `Backend::capabilities()`.
3519    //
3520    // Three properties hold on every backend, so they are not capabilities:
3521    // - Guest-visible wall and monotonic clocks stay in the same global
3522    //   virtual-time domain as timers, sleeps, and timeout deadlines. SaBRe's
3523    //   per-thread execution clock does not advance when the scheduler skips
3524    //   global time while a thread is blocked.
3525    // - A forked process child runs concurrently with its parent. Detcore
3526    //   blocks the parent only for CLONE_VFORK.
3527    // - Detcore dispatches every cloned thread's syscalls through its tool.
3528    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
3529    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-845): Review backend-local guest clock observations.
3530    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-1013): Review backend child process execution ordering.
3531    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-1013): Review backend thread callback coverage.
3532    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-1122): Review concurrent KVM process-child scheduling.
3533    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
3534    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-3635): Review in-guest LiteInst killed-thread RPC cancellation.
3535    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
3536    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-1152): KVM defers the vfork child spawn, so the child
3537    // registers its vfork barrier only after the parent posts BlockedExternalContinue. ptrace keeps
3538    // the parent kernel-blocked until the child registers.
3539    config.backend = backend_capabilities(backend);
3540    // Whether the run's process signals are tool-controlled is not decided
3541    // here: Detcore installs the backend's process signal control when the
3542    // backend offers it and threads are sequentialized, and reads the installed
3543    // control from then on.
3544    // Only ptrace has been measured to report a thread's signal state in /proc
3545    // (https://github.com/rrnewton/hermit/issues/3146). It hands a restart errno
3546    // to the kernel's signal delivery at the syscall stop, which turns it into
3547    // `EINTR` or a restart as Linux does. Every other backend keeps the previous
3548    // blocking-wait behavior. That includes LiteInst, which now runs only
3549    // in-guest: it has no ptrace stop at which the kernel turns a restart errno
3550    // such as ERESTARTNOHAND into `EINTR` or a restart, so the value could
3551    // reach guest code. `reverie::BackendCapabilities` has no field for this
3552    // fact yet, and no combination of its fields selects ptrace alone (its
3553    // `E9PATCH` constant is `PTRACE`), so it is set here from the backend's name.
3554    config.backend_supports_blocked_wait_signal_interruption = backend == Backend::Ptrace;
3555    // Only those waits read it (`Config::guest_may_inherit_a_terminal`), so the
3556    // host is asked only for them.
3557    config.guest_may_inherit_a_terminal =
3558        config.backend_supports_blocked_wait_signal_interruption && launcher_may_pass_a_terminal();
3559    config
3560}
3561
3562/// Whether the guest may start with a terminal, which can make Linux send the
3563/// guest signals at moments the host sets, such as SIGHUP when the terminal
3564/// hangs up and SIGWINCH when it is resized
3565/// (`Config::guest_may_inherit_a_terminal`): one of this process's standard
3566/// descriptors, which the guest inherits, is a terminal, or this process has a
3567/// controlling terminal, which the guest inherits with its session because
3568/// neither Hermit nor Reverie starts a new session for it. When the
3569/// controlling terminal cannot be read, the answer is yes.
3570fn launcher_may_pass_a_terminal() -> bool {
3571    // SAFETY: isatty only inspects the descriptor.
3572    let standard_terminal = (0..=2).any(|fd| unsafe { libc::isatty(fd) } == 1);
3573    may_pass_a_terminal(standard_terminal, controlling_terminal_device())
3574}
3575
3576/// The rule of `launcher_may_pass_a_terminal`: a terminal standard descriptor,
3577/// a controlling terminal (a nonzero device number), or an unknown one.
3578fn may_pass_a_terminal(standard_terminal: bool, controlling_terminal: Option<i64>) -> bool {
3579    standard_terminal || controlling_terminal != Some(0)
3580}
3581
3582/// This process's controlling terminal as `/proc/self/stat` reports it: field
3583/// 7, `tty_nr`, the terminal's device number, or 0 when there is none. `None`
3584/// when it cannot be read.
3585fn controlling_terminal_device() -> Option<i64> {
3586    parse_controlling_terminal_device(&std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/self/stat").ok()?)
3587}
3588
3589/// `tty_nr` from the text of a `/proc/<pid>/stat` file. The command name in
3590/// field 2 is parenthesized and may hold spaces and parentheses, so the fields
3591/// after it are counted from its last `)`: state, ppid, pgrp, session, tty_nr.
3592fn parse_controlling_terminal_device(stat: &str) -> Option<i64> {
3593    let after_command = stat.get(stat.rfind(')')? + 1..)?;
3594    after_command.split_ascii_whitespace().nth(4)?.parse().ok()
3595}
3596
3597/// What the selected backend can do, as Detcore consumes it.
3598///
3599/// Backends whose crate is always linked are asked directly. The LiteInst and
3600/// DBT constants are checked against their crates by tests built with those
3601/// features; the SaBRe and E9patch values have no such check.
3602#[doc(hidden)]
3603pub fn backend_capabilities(backend: Backend) -> reverie::BackendCapabilities {
3604    match backend {
3605        Backend::Ptrace => <reverie_ptrace::PtraceBackend as reverie::Backend>::capabilities(),
3606        Backend::Kvm => reverie_kvm::KvmBackend::capabilities(),
3607        Backend::E9patch => {
3608            let mut capabilities = reverie::BackendCapabilities::E9PATCH;
3609            // This arm is reached only when a caller passes `Backend::E9patch`
3610            // to this helper directly. `hermit run --backend=e9patch` maps the
3611            // selection to `Backend::Ptrace` before it configures the run, so
3612            // that run takes ptrace's value. A direct caller has always seen
3613            // the robust-list transition left undeferred, and this keeps that.
3614            // TODO: decide whether this direct path should inherit ptrace's value.
3615            capabilities.runs_exit_robust_list = false;
3616            capabilities
3617        }
3618        // Site patching changes how a syscall reaches the Tool, not what the
3619        // Tool host can do, so in-guest-trap has in-guest LiteInst's capabilities.
3620        Backend::Liteinst | Backend::InGuestTrap => reverie::BackendCapabilities::LITEINST_IN_GUEST,
3621        Backend::Sabre => reverie::BackendCapabilities::SABRE,
3622        Backend::Dbt => reverie::BackendCapabilities::DBT,
3623    }
3624}
3625
3626/// After an in-guest LiteInst run, waits (bounded) until the watcher has
3627/// reported every admitted process's physical exit, then releases any exit
3628/// barrier still recorded. Release comes only after that drain: an exit still
3629/// in flight is reported first.
3630///
3631/// Every report is applied again to the run's returned state. The backend
3632/// lets go of its own reference to that state when the run ends, and a report
3633/// that arrived afterwards reached nothing; applying a report twice consumes
3634/// nothing the second time.
3635///
3636/// A failed drain, a fired watchdog or a refused admission fails the run:
3637/// it records a determinism loss, so verification refuses to compare it, and
3638/// returns the failure, after the barriers are released.
3639#[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
3640async fn settle_in_guest_exits(
3641    exits: &in_guest_exits::InGuestExitAdmission,
3642    global_state: &detcore::GlobalState,
3643) -> Result<(), Error> {
3644    let drained = exits.drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10)).await;
3645    for pid in exits.reported_exits() {
3646        global_state.complete_physical_process_exit(pid);
3647    }
3648    global_state.release_all_physical_process_exits();
3649    let failure = exits.failure().or_else(|| {
3650        drained
3651            .err()
3652            .map(|error| format!("not every in-guest LiteInst guest exit was reported: {error:?}"))
3653    });
3654    match failure {
3655        Some(reason) => {
3656            detcore::detlog::record_determinism_loss(&reason);
3657            Err(Error::msg(reason))
3658        }
3659        None => Ok(()),
3660    }
3661}
3662
3663// TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-736): Review reserved LiteInst runtime failure statuses.
3664//
3665// ⚠️ THIS PREDICATE AND THE CLASSIFIER MUST AGREE ABOUT THE SIGNAL BAND, AND A
3666// HARD-CODED RANGE CANNOT. They do NOT cover the same statuses and are not meant
3667// to -- see the table below for what each covers. It is the SIGNAL half that has
3668// to move together, because both readers derive it from the same fact. When the reserved set grew to include the signal
3669// band, `Exited(130)` fell outside `122..=127` and is NOT `Signaled(_, _)` --
3670// it is a status hermit CHOSE, not an actual signal death -- so LiteInst
3671// skipped the forced shutdown and `clean_up` could wait forever on
3672// `handle.await` (detcore/src/tool_global.rs). The reserved set had grown past
3673// the range this hard-coded, and nothing connected the two.
3674//
3675// Keying the SIGNAL HALF on `signal_from_exit_status` -- the same predicate
3676// `classify_container_result` uses -- is what stops that half drifting again: a
3677// change to the band moves both readers or neither.
3678//
3679// ⚠️ THE TWO PREDICATES ARE NOT THE SAME SET, AND AN EARLIER VERSION OF THIS
3680// COMMENT SAID THEY WERE. agent(hermit-007)'s codex lane caught the overclaim.
3681// They deliberately differ:
3682//
3683//   this predicate            122..=127        +  signal band  +  real Signaled
3684//   classify_container_result 122 + 123 + 124  +  signal band
3685//
3686// 123..=127 force a LiteInst shutdown and are classified as NEITHER a refusal
3687// nor a signal death. The classifier gives 123 and 124 arms of their own -- 123
3688// is the log byte cap (hermit's `--max-log-bytes`, and the value reserved for
3689// safehermit's log-cap kill, which still exits 125), 124 a deadline hermit
3690// enforced -- while 125 (hermit itself) and 126/127 (exec-level) have none, and
3691// each has a different producer. Only the signal half is shared, and only the
3692// signal half is protected from drift. Do not "simplify" this by assuming the
3693// two agree; the `122..=127` bound here is still hard-coded and still needs a
3694// human to widen it if the reserved set grows again.
3695#[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
3696fn liteinst_requires_forced_shutdown(status: ExitStatus) -> bool {
3697    match status {
3698        // A real signal death: the process never chose a status at all.
3699        ExitStatus::Signaled(_, _) => true,
3700        ExitStatus::Exited(code) => {
3701            // The 122..=127 reserved band, plus any status hermit emits to mean
3702            // "killed by signal N".
3703            (122..=127).contains(&code) || detcore_model::signal_from_exit_status(code).is_some()
3704        }
3705    }
3706}
3707
3708/// The guest outlived the wall-clock bound the caller asked hermit to enforce.
3709///
3710/// A DISTINCT TYPE, NOT A STRING, because `failure_exit_code` and
3711/// `classify_failure` both have to recognise it and a prose match would break
3712/// silently the first time the wording changed.
3713#[derive(Debug)]
3714pub struct GuestTimedOut {
3715    /// The bound that was exceeded, as the caller spelled it.
3716    pub limit: Duration,
3717}
3718
3719impl std::fmt::Display for GuestTimedOut {
3720    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
3721        write!(
3722            f,
3723            "Guest exceeded the --timeout bound of {} seconds",
3724            self.limit.as_secs()
3725        )
3726    }
3727}
3728
3729impl std::error::Error for GuestTimedOut {}
3730
3731/// Nonordinary backends retain their existing cooperative timeout behavior.
3732/// There is no process-wide alarm in this library. A caller needing a bound on
3733/// synchronous stalls must own external supervision; the CLI arms its own
3734/// separate init-process fallback before invoking the library. Ordinary ptrace
3735/// uses the retained whole-operation driver instead of cancelling this future.
3736async fn with_run_deadline<F>(timeout: Option<Duration>, guest: F) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error>
3737where
3738    F: std::future::Future<Output = Result<ExitStatus, Error>>,
3739{
3740    match timeout {
3741        None => guest.await,
3742        Some(limit) => match tokio::time::timeout(limit, guest).await {
3743            Ok(result) => result,
3744            Err(_) => Err(Error::new(GuestTimedOut { limit })),
3745        },
3746    }
3747}
3748
3749#[tokio::main(flavor = "current_thread")]
3750async fn run_with_backend_inner(
3751    command: Command,
3752    config: DetConfig,
3753    print_summary: bool,
3754    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3755    backend: Backend,
3756    timeout: Option<Duration>,
3757) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
3758    refuse_in_guest_liteinst_timeout(backend, timeout)?;
3759    // Keep the large backend future off the container supervisor's stack
3760    // before the deadline and Tokio wrappers capture it. Polling and dropping
3761    // stay inline, including the backend teardown on timeout.
3762    let guest = Box::pin(dispatch_backend(
3763        command,
3764        config,
3765        print_summary,
3766        print_summary_to_json_file,
3767        backend,
3768        None,
3769    ));
3770    with_run_deadline(timeout, guest).await
3771}
3772
3773async fn dispatch_backend(
3774    command: Command,
3775    config: DetConfig,
3776    print_summary: bool,
3777    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3778    backend: Backend,
3779    control: Option<std::rc::Rc<ptrace_completion::Control>>,
3780) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
3781    if backend == Backend::Kvm {
3782        return Ok(run_kvm(
3783            &command,
3784            config,
3785            print_summary,
3786            print_summary_to_json_file,
3787            false,
3788        )
3789        .await?
3790        .status);
3791    }
3792    if backend == Backend::Dbt {
3793        #[cfg(feature = "dbt")]
3794        {
3795            return Ok(run_dbt(command, config, print_summary, false).await?.status);
3796        }
3797        #[cfg(not(feature = "dbt"))]
3798        {
3799            backend.ensure_available()?;
3800            unreachable!("DBT availability must fail when the feature is disabled");
3801        }
3802    }
3803    if backend == Backend::Sabre {
3804        return Ok(run_sabre(
3805            command,
3806            config,
3807            print_summary,
3808            print_summary_to_json_file,
3809            false,
3810        )
3811        .await?
3812        .status);
3813    }
3814    if backend.is_in_guest_liteinst() {
3815        #[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
3816        {
3817            let stats_request = in_guest_stats_request(backend, print_summary_to_json_file);
3818            let mut command = command;
3819            refuse_in_guest_liteinst_run(&mut command, &config, backend)?;
3820            announce_liteinst_config_fingerprint(&mut command);
3821            let _detlog_descriptor = request_liteinst_detlog_forwarding(
3822                &mut command,
3823                &in_guest_detlog_forward_policy(),
3824            )?;
3825            // Every Tool image must forward on that socket, by that policy;
3826            // see detcore::Config::in_guest_detlog_forward_policy.
3827            let mut config = config;
3828            config.in_guest_detlog_forward_policy = _detlog_descriptor
3829                .is_some()
3830                .then(|| in_guest_detlog_forward_policy().encode());
3831            config.in_guest_site_patching_off = in_guest_site_patching_off(backend);
3832            let preload = liteinst_tool_runtime_library_path()?;
3833            let exits = std::sync::Arc::new(in_guest_exits::InGuestExitAdmission::default());
3834            let (exit_status, mut global_state, dispatch_stats, process_reports) =
3835                reverie_liteinst::LiteinstBackend::with_connection_admission(
3836                    exits.clone(),
3837                    async {
3838                        if stats_request.is_enabled() {
3839                            let (exit_status, global_state, source) =
3840                                reverie_liteinst::LiteinstBackend::run_with_preload_and_stats::<
3841                                    Detcore,
3842                                >(command, config, preload)
3843                                .await?;
3844                            let dispatch_stats =
3845                                backend_stats::report(backend, stats_request, &source);
3846                            let process_reports = source.snapshot().process_reports();
3847                            Ok::<_, reverie::Error>((
3848                                exit_status,
3849                                global_state,
3850                                dispatch_stats,
3851                                process_reports,
3852                            ))
3853                        } else {
3854                            let (exit_status, global_state) =
3855                                reverie_liteinst::LiteinstBackend::run_with_preload::<Detcore>(
3856                                    command, config, preload,
3857                                )
3858                                .await?;
3859                            Ok((exit_status, global_state, None, 0))
3860                        }
3861                    },
3862                )
3863                .await
3864                .inspect_err(|error| exits.fail_launch(error))?;
3865            let patched =
3866                in_guest_trap_patching_evidence(backend, dispatch_stats.as_ref(), process_reports);
3867            let settled = settle_in_guest_exits(&exits, &global_state).await;
3868            if settled.is_err() || liteinst_requires_forced_shutdown(exit_status) {
3869                global_state.force_shutdown_with_error();
3870                global_state.cancel_internal_scheduler().await;
3871            }
3872            global_state
3873                .clean_up_with_dispatch_stats(
3874                    print_summary,
3875                    print_summary_to_json_file,
3876                    dispatch_stats,
3877                )
3878                .await;
3879            settled?;
3880            refuse_in_guest_trap_patching(backend, patched)?;
3881            return Ok(exit_status);
3882        }
3883        #[cfg(not(feature = "liteinst"))]
3884        {
3885            backend.ensure_available()?;
3886            unreachable!("LiteInst availability must fail when the feature is disabled");
3887        }
3888    }
3889    ensure_backend_dispatch(backend)?;
3890
3891    let stats_request = backend_stats::request(print_summary_to_json_file);
3892    let mut builder = reverie_ptrace::TracerBuilder::<Detcore>::new(command).config(config.clone());
3893    if config.gdbserver {
3894        builder = builder.gdbserver(config.gdbserver_port);
3895        if config.sequentialize_threads {
3896            // Inform gdbserver not to serialize guests because this is
3897            // done by detcore already. Without this the gdbserver freezes
3898            // the other threads around a breakpoint stop and waits for each
3899            // to report, but under detcore they are parked in its scheduler
3900            // rather than blocked in the kernel, so they cannot answer until
3901            // the scheduler runs them -- which it cannot while the stopped
3902            // thread holds its turn. The replay path already does this.
3903            builder = builder.sequentialized_guest();
3904        }
3905    }
3906    let control = control
3907        .ok_or_else(|| Error::msg("ordinary ptrace requires its retained operation owner"))?;
3908    let tracer = builder.backend_stats(stats_request).spawn().await?;
3909    let stats_source = tracer.backend_stats();
3910    let (exit_status, global_state) = ptrace_completion::wait(tracer, control).await?;
3911    let dispatch_stats =
3912        backend_stats::report_if_collected(backend, stats_request, stats_source.as_ref());
3913    global_state
3914        .clean_up_with_dispatch_stats(print_summary, print_summary_to_json_file, dispatch_stats)
3915        .await; // Before it's dropped by this function.
3916    Ok(exit_status)
3917}
3918
3919/// Variant of `run` that also captures stdout/stderr.
3920pub fn run_with_output(
3921    command: Command,
3922    config: DetConfig,
3923    print_summary: bool,
3924    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3925) -> Result<Output, Error> {
3926    run_with_output_backend(
3927        command,
3928        config,
3929        print_summary,
3930        print_summary_to_json_file,
3931        Backend::Ptrace,
3932    )
3933}
3934
3935/// Variant of [`run_with_backend`] that also captures stdout/stderr.
3936pub fn run_with_output_backend(
3937    command: Command,
3938    config: DetConfig,
3939    print_summary: bool,
3940    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3941    backend: Backend,
3942) -> Result<Output, Error> {
3943    if backend == Backend::Kvm {
3944        ensure_kvm_stdin_reserved()?;
3945    }
3946    run_with_output_backend_timeout(
3947        command,
3948        config,
3949        print_summary,
3950        print_summary_to_json_file,
3951        backend,
3952        None,
3953    )
3954}
3955
3956/// [`run_with_output_backend`] with a hermit-enforced wall-clock bound.
3957///
3958/// See [`run_with_backend_timeout`]; the teardown semantics are identical.
3959pub fn run_with_output_backend_timeout(
3960    command: Command,
3961    config: DetConfig,
3962    print_summary: bool,
3963    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
3964    backend: Backend,
3965    timeout: Option<Duration>,
3966) -> Result<Output, Error> {
3967    if backend == Backend::Ptrace {
3968        let summary_path = print_summary_to_json_file.clone();
3969        return ptrace_completion::run(timeout, move |control| async move {
3970            let report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
3971            let result = dispatch_output_backend(
3972                command,
3973                prepare_backend_config(config, backend),
3974                print_summary,
3975                &summary_path,
3976                backend,
3977                Some(control),
3978            )
3979            .await;
3980            report.finish(result)
3981        });
3982    }
3983    let (output, skid_overshoots) = run_with_output_backend_timeout_and_skid_overshoots(
3984        command,
3985        config,
3986        print_summary,
3987        print_summary_to_json_file,
3988        backend,
3989        timeout,
3990    )?;
3991    if skid_overshoots > 0 {
3992        return Err(Error::new(SkidOvershootError::new(skid_overshoots)));
3993    }
3994    Ok(output)
3995}
3996
3997/// Run with captured output and return the number of precise PMU timer
3998/// overshoots alongside it instead of refusing before a caller can inspect the
3999/// completed output. The `--verify` path uses this to finish and publish its
4000/// comparison before it refuses the overshoot-tainted result.
4001#[doc(hidden)]
4002pub fn run_with_output_backend_timeout_and_skid_overshoots(
4003    command: Command,
4004    config: DetConfig,
4005    print_summary: bool,
4006    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
4007    backend: Backend,
4008    timeout: Option<Duration>,
4009) -> Result<(Output, u64), Error> {
4010    if backend == Backend::Ptrace {
4011        let summary_path = print_summary_to_json_file.clone();
4012        return ptrace_completion::run(timeout, move |control| async move {
4013            let report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4014            let result = dispatch_output_backend(
4015                command,
4016                prepare_backend_config(config, backend),
4017                print_summary,
4018                &summary_path,
4019                backend,
4020                Some(control),
4021            )
4022            .await;
4023            report.finish_with_count(result)
4024        });
4025    }
4026    let skid_overshoot_report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(backend.uses_ptrace_pmu_timers());
4027    if backend == Backend::Kvm {
4028        // Reserve before the Tokio runtime can reuse a closed fd 0. KVM
4029        // verification consumes the explicit output snapshot in `run_kvm`;
4030        // public output-capture callers without one retain this reservation.
4031        ensure_kvm_stdin_reserved()?;
4032    }
4033    let config = prepare_backend_config(config, backend);
4034    let result = run_with_output_backend_inner(
4035        command,
4036        config,
4037        print_summary,
4038        print_summary_to_json_file,
4039        backend,
4040        timeout,
4041    );
4042    skid_overshoot_report.finish_with_count(result)
4043}
4044
4045#[tokio::main(flavor = "current_thread")]
4046async fn run_with_output_backend_inner(
4047    command: Command,
4048    config: DetConfig,
4049    print_summary: bool,
4050    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
4051    backend: Backend,
4052    timeout: Option<Duration>,
4053) -> Result<Output, Error> {
4054    refuse_in_guest_liteinst_timeout(backend, timeout)?;
4055    let Some(limit) = timeout else {
4056        return dispatch_output_backend(
4057            command,
4058            config,
4059            print_summary,
4060            print_summary_to_json_file,
4061            backend,
4062            None,
4063        )
4064        .await;
4065    };
4066    match tokio::time::timeout(
4067        limit,
4068        dispatch_output_backend(
4069            command,
4070            config,
4071            print_summary,
4072            print_summary_to_json_file,
4073            backend,
4074            None,
4075        ),
4076    )
4077    .await
4078    {
4079        Ok(result) => result,
4080        Err(_elapsed) => Err(Error::new(GuestTimedOut { limit })),
4081    }
4082}
4083
4084async fn dispatch_output_backend(
4085    mut command: Command,
4086    config: DetConfig,
4087    print_summary: bool,
4088    print_summary_to_json_file: &Option<PathBuf>,
4089    backend: Backend,
4090    control: Option<std::rc::Rc<ptrace_completion::Control>>,
4091) -> Result<Output, Error> {
4092    if backend == Backend::Kvm {
4093        return run_kvm(
4094            &command,
4095            config,
4096            print_summary,
4097            print_summary_to_json_file,
4098            true,
4099        )
4100        .await;
4101    }
4102    if backend == Backend::Dbt {
4103        #[cfg(feature = "dbt")]
4104        {
4105            return run_dbt(command, config, print_summary, true).await;
4106        }
4107        #[cfg(not(feature = "dbt"))]
4108        {
4109            backend.ensure_available()?;
4110            unreachable!("DBT availability must fail when the feature is disabled");
4111        }
4112    }
4113    if backend == Backend::Sabre {
4114        #[cfg(not(feature = "sabre"))]
4115        backend.ensure_available()?;
4116        command.stdin(output_backend_stdin()?);
4117        command.stdout(Stdio::piped());
4118        command.stderr(Stdio::piped());
4119        return run_sabre(
4120            command,
4121            config,
4122            print_summary,
4123            print_summary_to_json_file,
4124            true,
4125        )
4126        .await;
4127    }
4128    if backend.is_in_guest_liteinst() {
4129        #[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
4130        {
4131            command.stdin(output_backend_stdin()?);
4132            let stats_request = in_guest_stats_request(backend, print_summary_to_json_file);
4133            refuse_in_guest_liteinst_run(&mut command, &config, backend)?;
4134            announce_liteinst_config_fingerprint(&mut command);
4135            let _detlog_descriptor = request_liteinst_detlog_forwarding(
4136                &mut command,
4137                &in_guest_detlog_forward_policy(),
4138            )?;
4139            // Every Tool image must forward on that socket, by that policy;
4140            // see detcore::Config::in_guest_detlog_forward_policy.
4141            let mut config = config;
4142            config.in_guest_detlog_forward_policy = _detlog_descriptor
4143                .is_some()
4144                .then(|| in_guest_detlog_forward_policy().encode());
4145            config.in_guest_site_patching_off = in_guest_site_patching_off(backend);
4146            let preload = liteinst_tool_runtime_library_path()?;
4147            let exits = std::sync::Arc::new(in_guest_exits::InGuestExitAdmission::default());
4148            let (output, mut global_state, dispatch_stats, process_reports) =
4149                reverie_liteinst::LiteinstBackend::with_connection_admission(exits.clone(), async {
4150                    if stats_request.is_enabled() {
4151                        let (output, global_state, source) =
4152                            reverie_liteinst::LiteinstBackend::run_with_output_and_preload_and_stats::<
4153                                Detcore,
4154                            >(command, config, preload)
4155                            .await?;
4156                        let dispatch_stats = backend_stats::report(backend, stats_request, &source);
4157                        let process_reports = source.snapshot().process_reports();
4158                        Ok::<_, reverie::Error>((output, global_state, dispatch_stats, process_reports))
4159                    } else {
4160                        let (output, global_state) =
4161                            reverie_liteinst::LiteinstBackend::run_with_output_and_preload::<Detcore>(
4162                                command, config, preload,
4163                            )
4164                            .await?;
4165                        Ok((output, global_state, None, 0))
4166                    }
4167                })
4168                .await
4169                .inspect_err(|error| exits.fail_launch(error))?;
4170            let patched =
4171                in_guest_trap_patching_evidence(backend, dispatch_stats.as_ref(), process_reports);
4172            let settled = settle_in_guest_exits(&exits, &global_state).await;
4173            let output = Output {
4174                status: output.status.into(),
4175                stdout: output.stdout,
4176                stderr: output.stderr,
4177            };
4178            let status = output.status;
4179            if settled.is_err() || liteinst_requires_forced_shutdown(status) {
4180                global_state.force_shutdown_with_error();
4181                global_state.cancel_internal_scheduler().await;
4182            }
4183            global_state
4184                .clean_up_with_dispatch_stats(
4185                    print_summary,
4186                    print_summary_to_json_file,
4187                    dispatch_stats,
4188                )
4189                .await;
4190            settled?;
4191            refuse_in_guest_trap_patching(backend, patched)?;
4192            return Ok(Output {
4193                status,
4194                stdout: output.stdout,
4195                stderr: output.stderr,
4196            });
4197        }
4198        #[cfg(not(feature = "liteinst"))]
4199        {
4200            backend.ensure_available()?;
4201            unreachable!("LiteInst availability must fail when the feature is disabled");
4202        }
4203    }
4204    ensure_backend_dispatch(backend)?;
4205
4206    let stats_request = backend_stats::request(print_summary_to_json_file);
4207    command.stdin(output_backend_stdin()?);
4208    command.stdout(Stdio::piped());
4209    command.stderr(Stdio::piped());
4210    let mut builder = reverie_ptrace::TracerBuilder::<Detcore>::new(command).config(config.clone());
4211    if config.gdbserver {
4212        builder = builder.gdbserver(config.gdbserver_port);
4213        if config.sequentialize_threads {
4214            // Inform gdbserver not to serialize guests because this is
4215            // done by detcore already. Without this the gdbserver freezes
4216            // the other threads around a breakpoint stop and waits for each
4217            // to report, but under detcore they are parked in its scheduler
4218            // rather than blocked in the kernel, so they cannot answer until
4219            // the scheduler runs them -- which it cannot while the stopped
4220            // thread holds its turn. The replay path already does this.
4221            builder = builder.sequentialized_guest();
4222        }
4223    }
4224    let control = control
4225        .ok_or_else(|| Error::msg("ordinary ptrace requires its retained operation owner"))?;
4226    let tracer = builder.backend_stats(stats_request).spawn().await?;
4227    let stats_source = tracer.backend_stats();
4228    let (output, global_state) = ptrace_completion::wait_with_output(tracer, control).await?;
4229    let dispatch_stats =
4230        backend_stats::report_if_collected(backend, stats_request, stats_source.as_ref());
4231    global_state
4232        .clean_up_with_dispatch_stats(print_summary, print_summary_to_json_file, dispatch_stats)
4233        .await;
4234    Ok(output)
4235}
4236
4237/// Holds the context necessary to run high-level hermit functions.
4238pub struct HermitData {
4239    // The data directory. Defaults to `~/.cache/hermit`. Note that we shouldn't
4240    // expect this to exist in any of the functions that are called.
4241    data_dir: PathBuf,
4242}
4243
4244fn collect_recording_ids(
4245    entries: impl IntoIterator<Item = io::Result<fs::DirEntry>>,
4246    data_dir: &Path,
4247    mut file_type: impl FnMut(&fs::DirEntry) -> io::Result<fs::FileType>,
4248) -> Result<Vec<Id>, Error> {
4249    let mut recordings = Vec::new();
4250    for entry in entries {
4251        let entry = entry.with_context(|| {
4252            format!(
4253                "Failed to read an entry in recording inventory {}",
4254                data_dir.display()
4255            )
4256        })?;
4257        let path = entry.path();
4258        if file_type(&entry)
4259            .with_context(|| {
4260                format!(
4261                    "Failed to inspect recording inventory entry {}",
4262                    path.display()
4263                )
4264            })?
4265            .is_dir()
4266            && let Some(id) = entry
4267                .file_name()
4268                .to_str()
4269                .and_then(|name| name.parse::<Id>().ok())
4270        {
4271            recordings.push(id);
4272        }
4273    }
4274    Ok(recordings)
4275}
4276
4277impl Default for HermitData {
4278    fn default() -> Self {
4279        Self::new()
4280    }
4281}
4282
4283impl HermitData {
4284    /// Creates an instance of `HermitData` using `~/.cache/hermit` as the data
4285    /// directory.
4286    pub fn new() -> Self {
4287        Self::with_dir(
4288            dirs::cache_dir()
4289                .map_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("/tmp/hermit"), |dir| dir.join("hermit")),
4290        )
4291    }
4292
4293    /// Creates a `HermitData` using the given directory as the base path for
4294    /// storing recording data.
4295    pub fn with_dir<P>(data_dir: P) -> Self
4296    where
4297        P: Into<PathBuf>,
4298    {
4299        Self {
4300            data_dir: data_dir.into(),
4301        }
4302    }
4303
4304    /// Returns the path to the data directory where recordings are stored.
4305    pub fn data_dir(&self) -> &Path {
4306        &self.data_dir
4307    }
4308
4309    /// Records the execution of the given command, returning its `Recording`.
4310    ///
4311    /// If recording failed, then an error is returned. Note that if the command
4312    /// itself failed, then we still return a successful recording, but its exit
4313    /// status will be non-zero.
4314    ///
4315    /// This low-level API does not create a Hermit container, so it has no
4316    /// authenticated private-mount provenance to supply. Detcore derives mount
4317    /// identities from the completed guest namespace. CLI container callers
4318    /// with producer-owned root provenance must use
4319    /// [`Self::record_with_mountinfo`].
4320    pub fn record(&self, command: Command) -> Result<Recording, Error> {
4321        let store = Self {
4322            data_dir: self.data_dir.clone(),
4323        };
4324        ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4325            let data = store.create_recording_dir()?;
4326            let exit_status =
4327                record_to_async(command, data.path(), Vec::new(), None, None, control).await?;
4328            store.commit_recording(data, exit_status)
4329        })
4330    }
4331
4332    /// Records with mountinfo provenance captured from the active container.
4333    pub fn record_with_mountinfo(
4334        &self,
4335        command: Command,
4336        mountinfo_root_rewrites: Vec<detcore_model::config::MountInfoRootRewrite>,
4337    ) -> Result<Recording, Error> {
4338        let store = Self {
4339            data_dir: self.data_dir.clone(),
4340        };
4341        ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4342            let data = store.create_recording_dir()?;
4343            let mount_ids = capture_mountinfo_identity_order()?;
4344            let exit_status = record_to_async(
4345                command,
4346                data.path(),
4347                mountinfo_root_rewrites,
4348                Some(mount_ids),
4349                None,
4350                control,
4351            )
4352            .await?;
4353            store.commit_recording(data, exit_status)
4354        })
4355    }
4356
4357    /// Creates a temporary directory for a recording that has not been committed yet.
4358    pub fn create_recording_dir(&self) -> Result<tempfile::TempDir, Error> {
4359        let tmp_data_dir = self.data_dir.join("tmp");
4360
4361        fs::create_dir_all(&tmp_data_dir).with_context(|| {
4362            format!(
4363                "Failed to create recording directory: {}",
4364                self.data_dir.display()
4365            )
4366        })?;
4367
4368        Ok(tempfile::TempDir::new_in(tmp_data_dir)?)
4369    }
4370
4371    /// Commits a completed temporary recording to the recording store.
4372    pub fn commit_recording(
4373        &self,
4374        data: tempfile::TempDir,
4375        exit_status: ExitStatus,
4376    ) -> Result<Recording, Error> {
4377        let id = Id::unique();
4378
4379        // Atomically move the temporary recording to its final location.
4380        fs::rename(data.keep(), self.data_dir.join(id.to_string()))?;
4381
4382        self.update_last_id(&id)
4383            .with_context(|| format!("Failed to update {:?}", self.data_dir.join("last")))?;
4384
4385        Ok(Recording { id, exit_status })
4386    }
4387
4388    /// Replays the given recording ID.
4389    pub fn replay(&self, id: Id) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4390        let data = self.data_dir.join(id.to_string());
4391        replay_from(&data)
4392    }
4393
4394    /// Replays the given recording ID with a gdbserver available to attach to.
4395    pub fn replay_with_gdbserver(&self, id: Id, port: u16) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4396        let data = self.data_dir.join(id.to_string());
4397        replay_with_gdbserver(&data, port)
4398    }
4399
4400    /// Returns an iterator over the recordings.
4401    ///
4402    /// Use [`Self::recording_metadata`] to get more information about a recording.
4403    pub fn recordings(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = Id> + use<> {
4404        fs::read_dir(&self.data_dir)
4405            .ok()
4406            .into_iter()
4407            .flatten()
4408            .filter_map(|entry| {
4409                let entry = entry.ok()?;
4410
4411                if entry.file_type().ok()?.is_dir() {
4412                    Some(entry.file_name().to_str()?.parse::<Id>().ok()?)
4413                } else {
4414                    None
4415                }
4416            })
4417    }
4418
4419    /// Returns all recordings, or an error if the data directory cannot be
4420    /// enumerated completely.
4421    ///
4422    /// A missing data directory has no recordings. Other filesystem errors are
4423    /// returned rather than being mistaken for an empty or partial inventory.
4424    pub fn try_recordings(&self) -> Result<Vec<Id>, Error> {
4425        let entries = match fs::read_dir(&self.data_dir) {
4426            Ok(entries) => entries,
4427            Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(Vec::new()),
4428            Err(error) => {
4429                return Err(error).with_context(|| {
4430                    format!(
4431                        "Failed to read recording inventory in {}",
4432                        self.data_dir.display()
4433                    )
4434                });
4435            }
4436        };
4437
4438        collect_recording_ids(entries, &self.data_dir, fs::DirEntry::file_type)
4439    }
4440
4441    /// Returns the metadata of a recording.
4442    pub fn recording_metadata(&self, id: Id) -> Result<Metadata, Error> {
4443        Metadata::load(&self.data_dir.join(id.to_string()))
4444    }
4445
4446    /// Deletes a recording.
4447    pub fn remove(&self, id: Id) -> Result<(), Error> {
4448        let path = self.data_dir.join(id.to_string());
4449
4450        // Before deleting anything, make sure this file exists. This may not be a
4451        // recording if this file does not exist.
4452        let metadata_path = path.join(METADATA_NAME);
4453        let metadata = fs::metadata(&metadata_path)
4454            .with_context(|| format!("Failed to find {:?}", metadata_path))?;
4455
4456        if !metadata.is_file() {
4457            return Err(anyhow!("{:?} is not a file", metadata_path));
4458        }
4459
4460        // Do a recursive delete on the directory. Note that this does not follow
4461        // symlinks.
4462        fs::remove_dir_all(path)?;
4463
4464        Ok(())
4465    }
4466
4467    /// Returns the last recorded ID.
4468    pub fn last_id(&self) -> Result<Id, Error> {
4469        Ok(fs::read_to_string(self.data_dir.join("last"))?.parse()?)
4470    }
4471
4472    /// Atomically updates the last recording ID.
4473    fn update_last_id(&self, id: &Id) -> Result<(), Error> {
4474        let mut file = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new_in(self.data_dir.join("tmp"))?;
4475        write!(file, "{}", id)?;
4476        file.persist(self.data_dir.join("last"))?;
4477        Ok(())
4478    }
4479}
4480
4481/// Capture the current mount namespace's raw IDs in the exact row/parent order
4482/// used by Detcore's canonical mountinfo mapping.
4483pub fn capture_mountinfo_identity_order() -> Result<Vec<u64>, Error> {
4484    mountinfo_identity_order_from_raw(&fs::read("/proc/self/mountinfo")?)
4485}
4486
4487/// Producer provenance follows the guest mount model: ephemeral host seed rows
4488/// (other processes' squashfuse mounts) are not namespace members and must not
4489/// enter the captured identity order. Detcore drops the same rows with the same
4490/// filter when it captures the guest's mountinfo.
4491fn mountinfo_identity_order_from_raw(raw: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u64>, Error> {
4492    mountinfo_identity_order_from(&detcore_model::procfs::exclude_ephemeral_host_seed_mounts(
4493        raw,
4494    ))
4495}
4496
4497fn mountinfo_identity_order_from(contents: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u64>, Error> {
4498    use std::collections::BTreeSet;
4499
4500    let rows = detcore_model::procfs::parse_mountinfo(contents)
4501        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("malformed /proc/self/mountinfo"))?;
4502    let mut visible = Vec::new();
4503    let mut parents = Vec::new();
4504    let mut seen = BTreeSet::new();
4505    for row in rows {
4506        seen.insert(row.raw_mount_id);
4507        visible.push(row.raw_mount_id);
4508        parents.push(row.raw_parent_id);
4509    }
4510    for parent in parents {
4511        if seen.insert(parent) {
4512            visible.push(parent);
4513        }
4514    }
4515    Ok(visible)
4516}
4517
4518impl<'a> From<Option<&'a PathBuf>> for HermitData {
4519    fn from(data_dir: Option<&'a PathBuf>) -> Self {
4520        data_dir.map_or_else(Self::new, Self::with_dir)
4521    }
4522}
4523
4524/// Records to an existing directory. The caller must keep its backing resources
4525/// alive through any [`HermitCleanupUnconfirmed`] recovery. [`HermitData::record`]
4526/// owns its temporary recording directory inside the retained operation.
4527pub fn record_to(command: Command, dir: &Path) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4528    let dir = dir.to_owned();
4529    ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4530        record_to_async(command, &dir, Vec::new(), None, None, control).await
4531    })
4532}
4533
4534/// Records with producer-owned mountinfo provenance. The directory-lifetime
4535/// obligation is the same as [`record_to`]. `local_networking` is stored in
4536/// the recording's `metadata.json`, and an autopilot replay uses that network.
4537pub fn record_to_with_mountinfo(
4538    command: Command,
4539    dir: &Path,
4540    mountinfo_root_rewrites: Vec<detcore_model::config::MountInfoRootRewrite>,
4541    local_networking: bool,
4542) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4543    let dir = dir.to_owned();
4544    ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4545        let mount_ids = capture_mountinfo_identity_order()?;
4546        record_to_async(
4547            command,
4548            &dir,
4549            mountinfo_root_rewrites,
4550            Some(mount_ids),
4551            Some(local_networking),
4552            control,
4553        )
4554        .await
4555    })
4556}
4557
4558async fn record_to_async(
4559    command: Command,
4560    dir: &Path,
4561    mountinfo_root_rewrites: Vec<detcore_model::config::MountInfoRootRewrite>,
4562    mountinfo_mount_ids: Option<Vec<u64>>,
4563    local_networking: Option<bool>,
4564    control: std::rc::Rc<ptrace_completion::Control>,
4565) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4566    let report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4567    let result = async {
4568        Record::spawn_with_mountinfo(
4569            command,
4570            dir,
4571            mountinfo_root_rewrites,
4572            mountinfo_mount_ids,
4573            local_networking,
4574        )
4575        .await?
4576        .wait(control)
4577        .await
4578    }
4579    .await;
4580    report.finish(result)
4581}
4582
4583/// Records and captures output. Keep the directory's backing resources alive
4584/// through any cleanup checkpoint, as for [`record_to`].
4585pub fn record_with_output(command: Command, dir: &Path) -> Result<Output, Error> {
4586    let dir = dir.to_owned();
4587    ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4588        record_with_output_async(command, &dir, Vec::new(), None, None, control).await
4589    })
4590}
4591
4592/// Records with captured output and exact mountinfo provenance.
4593/// `local_networking` is as for [`record_to_with_mountinfo`].
4594pub fn record_with_output_with_mountinfo(
4595    command: Command,
4596    dir: &Path,
4597    mountinfo_root_rewrites: Vec<detcore_model::config::MountInfoRootRewrite>,
4598    local_networking: bool,
4599) -> Result<Output, Error> {
4600    let dir = dir.to_owned();
4601    ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4602        let mount_ids = capture_mountinfo_identity_order()?;
4603        record_with_output_async(
4604            command,
4605            &dir,
4606            mountinfo_root_rewrites,
4607            Some(mount_ids),
4608            Some(local_networking),
4609            control,
4610        )
4611        .await
4612    })
4613}
4614
4615async fn record_with_output_async(
4616    mut command: Command,
4617    dir: &Path,
4618    mountinfo_root_rewrites: Vec<detcore_model::config::MountInfoRootRewrite>,
4619    mountinfo_mount_ids: Option<Vec<u64>>,
4620    local_networking: Option<bool>,
4621    control: std::rc::Rc<ptrace_completion::Control>,
4622) -> Result<Output, Error> {
4623    let report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4624    command.stdin(Stdio::null());
4625    command.stdout(Stdio::piped());
4626    command.stderr(Stdio::piped());
4627    let result = async {
4628        Record::spawn_with_mountinfo(
4629            command,
4630            dir,
4631            mountinfo_root_rewrites,
4632            mountinfo_mount_ids,
4633            local_networking,
4634        )
4635        .await?
4636        .wait_with_output(control)
4637        .await
4638    }
4639    .await;
4640    report.finish(result)
4641}
4642
4643/// Replays an existing recording. Materialization and chroot guards remain with
4644/// the original runtime through any cleanup checkpoint.
4645pub fn replay_from(dir: &Path) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4646    replay_plain(dir, None, &[])
4647}
4648
4649/// Replays with a gdb server.
4650pub fn replay_with_gdbserver(dir: &Path, port: u16) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4651    replay_plain(dir, Some(port), &[])
4652}
4653
4654/// Replays with a gdb server and mounts inside the replay chroot.
4655pub fn replay_with_gdbserver_and_mounts(
4656    dir: &Path,
4657    port: u16,
4658    mounts: &[Mount],
4659) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4660    replay_plain(dir, Some(port), mounts)
4661}
4662
4663fn replay_plain(dir: &Path, port: Option<u16>, mounts: &[Mount]) -> Result<ExitStatus, Error> {
4664    let dir = dir.to_owned();
4665    let mounts = mounts.to_vec();
4666    ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4667        let report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4668        let result = async {
4669            Replay::spawn(&dir, false, port, &mounts)
4670                .await?
4671                .wait(control)
4672                .await
4673        }
4674        .await;
4675        report.finish(result)
4676    })
4677}
4678
4679/// Replays with captured output.
4680pub fn replay_with_output(dir: &Path) -> Result<Output, Error> {
4681    replay_with_output_and_mounts(dir, &[])
4682}
4683
4684/// Replays with captured output and mounts inside the replay chroot.
4685pub fn replay_with_output_and_mounts(dir: &Path, mounts: &[Mount]) -> Result<Output, Error> {
4686    let dir = dir.to_owned();
4687    let mounts = mounts.to_vec();
4688    ptrace_completion::run(None, move |control| async move {
4689        let report = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4690        let result = async {
4691            Replay::spawn(&dir, true, None, &mounts)
4692                .await?
4693                .wait_with_output(control)
4694                .await
4695        }
4696        .await;
4697        report.finish(result)
4698    })
4699}

4701#[cfg(test)]
4702mod tests {
4703    use super::*;
4704
4705    static SKID_OVERSHOOT_TEST_LOCK: std::sync::Mutex<()> = std::sync::Mutex::new(());
4706
4707    /// The launch-time capture drops ephemeral host seed rows before ordering,
4708    /// so a seed's raw ID never enters the provenance Detcore checks guest
4709    /// views against, and every retained row keeps its place.
4710    #[test]
4711    fn identity_capture_excludes_host_seed_mounts_and_keeps_order() {
4712        let raw = b"10 1 8:1 / / rw - ext4 /dev/root rw\n\
4713                    76 10 0:50 / /mnt/xarfuse/uid-1/e62a203d-seed-nspid4026531836_cgpid16161-ns-4026531832 rw - fuse.squashfuse_ll squashfuse_ll rw\n\
4714                    20 10 0:7 / /test rw - tmpfs tmpfs rw\n\
4715                    30 10 0:51 / /mnt/xarfuse/stable-release rw - fuse.squashfuse_ll squashfuse_ll rw\n";
4716        let order = mountinfo_identity_order_from_raw(raw).unwrap();
4717        assert!(!order.contains(&76), "seed mount ID captured: {order:?}");
4718        assert_eq!(order, [10, 20, 30, 1]);
4719        assert_eq!(
4720            mountinfo_identity_order_from(raw).unwrap(),
4721            [10, 76, 20, 30, 1],
4722            "control: without the filter the seed ID is captured"
4723        );
4724    }
4725
4726    #[test]
4727    fn kvm_mountinfo_config_preserves_captured_provenance() {
4728        let rewrite = detcore_model::config::MountInfoRootRewrite {
4729            raw_mount_id: 20,
4730            deterministic_root: b"/".to_vec(),
4731            raw_root_prefix: Some(b"/private-source".to_vec()),
4732            deterministic_root_prefix: Some(b"/tmp".to_vec()),
4733            raw_mountpoint_prefix: Some(b"/private-source".to_vec()),
4734            deterministic_mountpoint_prefix: Some(b"/tmp".to_vec()),
4735        };
4736        let mut config = DetConfig {
4737            mountinfo_mount_ids: vec![10, 20, 30],
4738            mountinfo_mount_ids_captured: true,
4739            mount_id_assignment_order: vec![700, 701],
4740            mountinfo_root_rewrites: vec![rewrite],
4741            mountinfo_device_rewrites: vec![(libc::makedev(0, 1), libc::makedev(8, 1))],
4742            ..DetConfig::default()
4743        };
4744        let mut expected = config.clone();
4745        expected.mountinfo_device_rewrites.clear();
4746        let mut captures = 0;
4747        prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut config, || {
4748            captures += 1;
4749            mountinfo_identity_order_from(
4750                b"10 30 8:1 / / rw - ext4 /dev/root rw\n\
4751                  20 10 0:7 / /test rw - tmpfs tmpfs rw\n",
4752            )
4753        })
4754        .unwrap();
4755        assert_eq!(
4756            captures, 1,
4757            "captured provenance must be checked before any guest read"
4758        );
4759        assert_eq!(
4760            serde_json::to_value(&config).unwrap(),
4761            serde_json::to_value(&expected).unwrap(),
4762            "root rewrites, listed and assignment orders and every other setting must remain intact"
4763        );
4764
4765        let mut empty = DetConfig {
4766            mountinfo_mount_ids_captured: true,
4767            ..DetConfig::default()
4768        };
4769        let mut captures = 0;
4770        prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut empty, || {
4771            captures += 1;
4772            mountinfo_identity_order_from(b"")
4773        })
4774        .unwrap();
4775        assert_eq!(captures, 1);
4776        assert!(empty.mountinfo_mount_ids.is_empty());
4777        assert!(empty.mountinfo_mount_ids_captured);
4778
4779        // Both captures describe the same completed namespace. An ordered
4780        // subset is valid for some later guest views, but not this boundary.
4781        for actual in [
4782            vec![10, 20],
4783            vec![10, 20, 30, 40],
4784            vec![20, 10, 30],
4785            vec![10, 20, 31],
4786            vec![10, 20, 20, 30],
4787            vec![],
4788        ] {
4789            let mut stale = config.clone();
4790            stale.mountinfo_device_rewrites = vec![(libc::makedev(0, 1), libc::makedev(8, 1))];
4791            let before = serde_json::to_value(&stale).unwrap();
4792            let error = prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut stale, || Ok(actual)).unwrap_err();
4793            assert!(
4794                error
4795                    .to_string()
4796                    .contains("do not match the backend namespace")
4797            );
4798            assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&stale).unwrap(), before);
4799        }
4800        let before = serde_json::to_value(&empty).unwrap();
4801        let error = prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut empty, || Ok(vec![10])).unwrap_err();
4802        assert!(
4803            error
4804                .to_string()
4805                .contains("do not match the backend namespace")
4806        );
4807        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&empty).unwrap(), before);
4808    }
4809
4810    #[test]
4811    fn kvm_mountinfo_config_captures_validated_ambient_namespace() {
4812        let contents = b"80 20 8:1 / / rw - ext4 /dev/root rw\n\
4813                         10 80 0:7 / /test rw - tmpfs tmpfs rw\n";
4814        let mut config = DetConfig::default();
4815        let mut expected = config.clone();
4816        expected.mountinfo_mount_ids = vec![80, 10, 20];
4817        expected.mountinfo_mount_ids_captured = true;
4818        prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut config, || mountinfo_identity_order_from(contents))
4819            .unwrap();
4820        assert_eq!(
4821            serde_json::to_value(&config).unwrap(),
4822            serde_json::to_value(&expected).unwrap(),
4823            "visible rows precede parent-only IDs and no guest setting changes"
4824        );
4825        assert!(detcore_model::procfs::mount_ids_are_ordered_subset(
4826            &[80, 10],
4827            &config.mountinfo_mount_ids
4828        ));
4829        assert!(!detcore_model::procfs::mount_ids_are_ordered_subset(
4830            &[10, 80],
4831            &config.mountinfo_mount_ids
4832        ));
4833        assert!(!detcore_model::procfs::mount_ids_are_ordered_subset(
4834            &[80, 99],
4835            &config.mountinfo_mount_ids
4836        ));
4837        assert!(!detcore_model::procfs::mount_ids_are_ordered_subset(
4838            &[80, 80],
4839            &config.mountinfo_mount_ids
4840        ));
4841
4842        let mut empty = DetConfig::default();
4843        prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut empty, || mountinfo_identity_order_from(b"")).unwrap();
4844        assert!(empty.mountinfo_mount_ids.is_empty());
4845        assert!(empty.mountinfo_mount_ids_captured);
4846    }
4847
4848    #[test]
4849    fn kvm_mountinfo_config_refuses_missing_or_malformed_capture() {
4850        let valid = b"10 20 8:1 / / rw - ext4 /dev/root rw\n";
4851        for captured in [false, true] {
4852            let config = DetConfig {
4853                mountinfo_mount_ids: if captured { vec![10, 20] } else { vec![] },
4854                mountinfo_mount_ids_captured: captured,
4855                mount_id_assignment_order: if captured { vec![700, 701] } else { vec![] },
4856                mountinfo_device_rewrites: vec![(libc::makedev(0, 1), libc::makedev(8, 1))],
4857                ..DetConfig::default()
4858            };
4859            for contents in [
4860                b"malformed".as_slice(),
4861                b"10 20 8:1 / / rw - ext4 /dev/root rw\n10 20 8:1 / / rw - ext4 /dev/root rw\n",
4862                b"10 20 8:1 / / rw unknown:7 - ext4 /dev/root rw\n",
4863            ] {
4864                let mut candidate = config.clone();
4865                let before = serde_json::to_value(&candidate).unwrap();
4866                let error = prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut candidate, || {
4867                    mountinfo_identity_order_from(contents)
4868                })
4869                .unwrap_err();
4870                assert!(format!("{error:#}").contains("malformed /proc/self/mountinfo"));
4871                assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&candidate).unwrap(), before);
4872            }
4873            let mut candidate = config;
4874            let before = serde_json::to_value(&candidate).unwrap();
4875            let error = prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut candidate, || {
4876                Err(std::io::Error::from(std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied).into())
4877            })
4878            .unwrap_err();
4879            assert!(
4880                error
4881                    .downcast_ref::<std::io::Error>()
4882                    .is_some_and(|error| error.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::PermissionDenied)
4883            );
4884            assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&candidate).unwrap(), before);
4885        }
4886        assert_eq!(mountinfo_identity_order_from(valid).unwrap(), [10, 20]);
4887    }
4888
4889    #[test]
4890    fn kvm_mountinfo_config_refuses_uncaptured_provenance_vectors() {
4891        for (listed, assigned) in [(vec![10], vec![]), (vec![], vec![700])] {
4892            let mut config = DetConfig {
4893                mountinfo_mount_ids: listed,
4894                mount_id_assignment_order: assigned,
4895                ..DetConfig::default()
4896            };
4897            let before = serde_json::to_value(&config).unwrap();
4898            let error = prepare_kvm_mountinfo_config(&mut config, || {
4899                panic!("must not overwrite incomplete provenance")
4900            })
4901            .unwrap_err();
4902            assert!(error.to_string().contains("no producer-captured namespace"));
4903            assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&config).unwrap(), before);
4904        }
4905    }
4906
4907    #[test]
4908    fn skid_overshoot_report_covers_success_error_and_disabled_backends() {
4909        let _lock = SKID_OVERSHOOT_TEST_LOCK.lock().unwrap();
4910        let _ = reverie::take_skid_overshoot_count();
4911
4912        reverie::record_skid_overshoot();
4913        let empty_run = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4914        assert_eq!(
4915            reverie::take_skid_overshoot_count(),
4916            0,
4917            "begin must clear evidence from a previous invocation"
4918        );
4919        empty_run.finish(Ok::<_, Error>(())).unwrap();
4920
4921        let success = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4922        reverie::record_skid_overshoot();
4923        reverie::record_skid_overshoot();
4924        let error = success.finish(Ok::<_, Error>(7)).unwrap_err();
4925        assert_eq!(
4926            error
4927                .downcast_ref::<SkidOvershootError>()
4928                .expect("skid refusal must remain typed")
4929                .count(),
4930            2
4931        );
4932        assert!(
4933            error
4934                .to_string()
4935                .contains("observed 2 HERMIT_SKID_OVERSHOOT report(s)"),
4936            "{error:#}"
4937        );
4938        assert!(
4939            error
4940                .to_string()
4941                .contains("deterministic execution was not established"),
4942            "{error:#}"
4943        );
4944        assert_eq!(reverie::take_skid_overshoot_count(), 0);
4945
4946        let error = SkidOvershootReport::begin(true);
4947        reverie::record_skid_overshoot();
4948        let error = error
4949            .finish(Err::<(), _>(anyhow!("guest failed")))
4950            .unwrap_err();
4951        assert!(error.downcast_ref::<SkidOvershootError>().is_some());
4952        let error = format!("{error:#}");
4953        assert!(error.contains("guest failed"), "{error}");
4954        assert!(
4955            error.contains("observed 1 HERMIT_SKID_OVERSHOOT report(s)"),
4956            "{error}"
4957        );
4958        assert_eq!(reverie::take_skid_overshoot_count(), 0);
4959
4960        reverie::record_skid_overshoot();
4961        let disabled = SkidOvershootReport::begin(false);
4962        disabled.finish(Ok::<_, Error>(())).unwrap();
4963        assert_eq!(
4964            reverie::take_skid_overshoot_count(),
4965            1,
4966            "a backend that cannot produce ptrace PMU overshoots must not consume another run's evidence"
4967        );
4968    }

4970    #[test]
4971    fn only_ptrace_hosted_backends_consume_skid_overshoot_reports() {
4972        for backend in [Backend::Ptrace, Backend::E9patch] {
4973            assert!(backend.uses_ptrace_pmu_timers(), "{backend:?}");
4974        }
4975        for backend in [Backend::Dbt, Backend::Sabre, Backend::Kvm] {
4976            assert!(!backend.uses_ptrace_pmu_timers(), "{backend:?}");
4977        }
4978        // In-guest Detcore has no ptrace tracer to arm the PMU timer from.
4979        assert!(!Backend::Liteinst.uses_ptrace_pmu_timers());
4980        assert!(!Backend::InGuestTrap.uses_ptrace_pmu_timers());
4981    }

4983    #[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
4984    fn write_guest_program(directory: &Path, name: &str, bytes: &[u8], mode: u32) -> PathBuf {
4985        let path = directory.join(name);
4986        fs::write(&path, bytes).unwrap();
4987        fs::set_permissions(&path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(mode)).unwrap();
4988        // The kernel may clear a set-group-ID bit that this user cannot set;
4989        // a test that silently lost its bit would test nothing.
4990        assert_eq!(
4991            fs::metadata(&path).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o7777,
4992            mode,
4993            "{}",
4994            path.display()
4995        );
4996        path
4997    }
4998
4999    #[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
5000    fn shebang_to(interpreter: &Path) -> Vec<u8> {
5001        let mut script = b"#!".to_vec();
5002        script.extend_from_slice(interpreter.as_os_str().as_bytes());
5003        script.extend_from_slice(b" -e\necho script\n");
5004        script
5005    }

5025    #[test]
5026    #[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
5027    fn in_guest_liteinst_refuses_programs_that_start_before_the_runtime() {
5028        use interp::startup_fixtures::*;
5029
5030        let directory = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
5031        let cases: [(&str, Vec<u8>, &str); 4] = [
5032            (
5033                "static",
5034                elf(EM_X86_64, &[(PT_LOAD, b"code")]),
5035                "is statically linked",
5036            ),
5037            (
5038                "preinit",
5039                dynamic_executable(true),
5040                "has a DT_PREINIT_ARRAY",
5041            ),
5042            (
5043                "aarch64",
5044                elf(EM_AARCH64, &[(PT_INTERP, INTERP)]),
5045                "is not a 64-bit x86-64 ELF image",
5046            ),
5047            (
5048                "text",
5049                b"plain text, no #! line\n".to_vec(),
5050                "is neither an ELF executable nor a #! script",
5051            ),
5052        ];
5053        for (name, bytes, reason) in cases {
5054            let program = write_guest_program(directory.path(), name, &bytes, 0o755);
5055            let gap = in_guest_liteinst_program_gap(&mut Command::new(&program))
5056                .unwrap()
5057                .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{name} was not refused"));
5058            assert!(
5059                gap.contains(reason) && gap.contains(&program.display().to_string()),
5060                "{name}: {gap}"
5061            );
5062        }
5063    }

5065    /// A failure recorded during an in-guest run fails the settle step and
5066    /// latches a determinism loss, so verification refuses the run (Codex
5067    /// implementation review, HIGH 3 and 4).
5068    #[tokio::test]
5069    #[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
5070    async fn a_recorded_in_guest_failure_fails_the_run_and_latches_a_loss() {
5071        use std::os::fd::FromRawFd;
5072
5073        use reverie::GlobalTool;
5074        use reverie_rpc_transport::ConnectionAdmission;
5075
5076        let config = DetConfig::default().with_backend(|backend| {
5077            backend.process_exits_complete_asynchronously = true;
5078        });
5079        let global_state = detcore::GlobalState::init_global_state(&config).await;
5080        let exits = in_guest_exits::InGuestExitAdmission::default();
5081        // Admitting before any reporter is attached is refused and recorded.
5082        let fd = unsafe { libc::syscall(libc::SYS_pidfd_open, std::process::id() as i32, 0) };
5083        assert!(fd >= 0);
5084        let pidfd = unsafe { std::os::fd::OwnedFd::from_raw_fd(fd as i32) };
5085        assert!(exits.admit(pidfd).is_err());
5086        let error = settle_in_guest_exits(&exits, &global_state)
5087            .await
5088            .expect_err("a recorded failure must fail the run");
5089        assert!(
5090            error
5091                .to_string()
5092                .starts_with("in-guest LiteInst refused a guest connection:"),
5093            "{error}"
5094        );
5095        assert!(detcore::detlog::determinism_loss().is_some());
5096    }
