7306    /// The build scripts' pin reader, bracketed against the inputs that
7307    /// first-match-wins got wrong. Included rather than imported because the
7308    /// reader is build-script code with no library home.
7309    mod reverie_pin_reader {
7310        include!("../reverie_pin.rs");
7311
7312        const A: &str = "1111111111111111111111111111111111111111";
7313        const B: &str = "2222222222222222222222222222222222222222";
7314
7315        fn dep(rev: &str) -> String {
7316            format!(
7317                "reverie = {{ git = \"https://github.com/rrnewton/reverie.git\", rev = \"{rev}\" }}\n"
7318            )
7319        }
7320
7321        #[test]
7322        fn a_single_revision_is_read() {
7323            assert_eq!(parse_reverie_pin(&dep(A)).as_deref(), Some(A));
7324        }
7325
7326        #[test]
7327        fn agreeing_revisions_are_read_once() {
7328            let text = format!("{}{}", dep(A), dep(A));
7329            assert_eq!(parse_reverie_pin(&text).as_deref(), Some(A));
7330        }
7331
7332        /// A manifest halfway through a bump names two revisions. First-match-wins
7333        /// silently returned the first; the canonical rule refuses to resolve it.
7334        #[test]
7335        fn disagreeing_revisions_are_refused() {
7336            let text = format!("{}{}", dep(A), dep(B));
7337            assert_eq!(parse_reverie_pin(&text), None);
7338        }
7339
7340        /// The reported reproduction: ONE commented line above the live pin made
7341        /// the binary embed the comment's revision, so a correctly staged runtime
7342        /// would be refused by a guard naming a revision that was never the pin.
7343        #[test]
7344        fn a_commented_out_dependency_is_not_the_pin() {
7345            let text = format!("# {}{}", dep(B), dep(A));
7346            assert_eq!(
7347                parse_reverie_pin(&text).as_deref(),
7348                Some(A),
7349                "a commented-out revision must not win"
7350            );
7351        }
7352
7353        #[test]
7354        fn a_manifest_naming_no_revision_yields_none() {
7355            assert_eq!(parse_reverie_pin("[dependencies]\nlibc = \"0.2\"\n"), None);
7356        }
7357
7358        /// An unparseable Reverie line could be hiding a disagreement, so it makes
7359        /// the manifest ambiguous rather than being skipped as absent.
7360        #[test]
7361        fn an_unparseable_revision_is_ambiguous() {
7362            let text = format!(
7363                "{}reverie-x = {{ git = \"https://github.com/rrnewton/reverie.git\", rev = \"beef\" }}\n",
7364                dep(A)
7365            );
7366            assert_eq!(parse_reverie_pin(&text), None);
7367        }
7368    }
7369
7370    /// SaBRe's guest plugin has no subscriber, so it forwards DETLOG records by
7371    /// the policy the CLI hands it. For each filter the CLI builds, that policy
7372    /// must answer every emitting module as the CLI's own subscriber does,
7373    /// which is what each in-process `detlog!` callsite asks under ptrace.
7374    /// `warn,detcore::random=info` once yielded no forwarding at all, because
7375    /// only the generic `detcore` target was asked, so SaBRe dropped the
7376    /// AT_RANDOM and early getrandom records ptrace prints.
7377    #[cfg(feature = "sabre")]
7378    #[test]
7379    fn sabre_detlog_forwarding_answers_each_target_as_the_cli_filter_does() {
7380        use detcore::detlog::ForwardPolicy;
7381        use detcore::detlog::info_enabled_for_target;
7382        use tracing::level_filters::LevelFilter;
7383        use tracing_subscriber::layer::SubscriberExt;
7384
7385        use crate::liteinst_bootstrap::EffectiveFilter;
7386
7387        let targets = [
7388            "",
7389            "detcore",
7390            "detcore::random",
7391            "detcore::randomness",
7392            "detcore::tool_local",
7393            "detcore::scheduler",
7394            "detcore::scheduler::runqueue",
7395            "detcore::syscalls::files",
7396            "hermit",
7397            "warn",
7398        ];
7399        let policy_under = |raw: &str, level: LevelFilter| {
7400            let subscriber = tracing_subscriber::registry()
7401                .with(EffectiveFilter::from_directives_lossy(raw, level).into_filter());
7402            tracing::subscriber::with_default(subscriber, || {
7403                let policy = ForwardPolicy::from_current_subscriber(raw);
7404                let subscriber_says: Vec<bool> = targets
7405                    .iter()
7406                    .map(|target| info_enabled_for_target(target))
7407                    .collect();
7408                (policy, subscriber_says)
7409            })
7410        };
7411        for (raw, level) in [
7412            ("warn,detcore::random=info", LevelFilter::WARN),
7413            ("detcore::random=info", LevelFilter::WARN),
7414            ("detcore::random=warn", LevelFilter::INFO),
7415            (
7416                "warn,detcore=info,detcore::scheduler=off",
7417                LevelFilter::WARN,
7418            ),
7419            (
7420                "warn,detcore[work]=info,detcore::random=info",
7421                LevelFilter::WARN,
7422            ),
7423            ("", LevelFilter::INFO),
7424            ("", LevelFilter::WARN),
7425        ] {
7426            let (policy, subscriber_says) = policy_under(raw, level);
7427            for (target, enabled) in targets.iter().zip(subscriber_says) {
7428                assert_eq!(
7429                    policy.forwards(target),
7430                    enabled,
7431                    "RUST_LOG={raw:?} --log={level}: policy {policy:?} disagrees on {target:?}"
7432                );
7433            }
7434        }
7435
7436        let (scoped, subscriber_says) =
7437            policy_under("warn,detcore::random=info", LevelFilter::WARN);
7438        assert!(!subscriber_says[1], "the generic detcore target is off");
7439        assert!(scoped.forwards_any());
7440        assert!(scoped.forwards("detcore::random"));
7441        assert!(!scoped.forwards("detcore::tool_local"));
7442        assert_eq!(policy_under("", LevelFilter::INFO).0, ForwardPolicy::all());
7443        assert!(!policy_under("", LevelFilter::WARN).0.forwards_any());
7444    }
7445
7446    /// In-guest LiteInst gets the per-target policy it is given next to the
7447    /// forwarding descriptor, and a policy that forwards nothing requests no
7448    /// forwarding and clears both variables.
7449    #[cfg(feature = "liteinst")]
7450    #[test]
7451    fn liteinst_detlog_forwarding_hands_the_guest_its_per_target_policy() {
7452        use std::os::fd::AsRawFd;
7453
7454        use detcore::detlog::ForwardPolicy;
7455        use reverie::process::Command;
7456
7457        use super::LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_ENV;
7458        use super::LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_POLICY_ENV;
7459        use super::forward_in_guest_detlogs_to;
7460        use super::request_liteinst_detlog_forwarding;
7461
7462        let (sink, _peer) = std::os::unix::net::UnixDatagram::pair().unwrap();
7463        forward_in_guest_detlogs_to(sink.into());
7464        let env = |command: &Command, key: &str| {
7465            command
7466                .get_envs()
7467                .find(|(name, _)| *name == std::ffi::OsStr::new(key))
7468                .map(|(_, value)| value.map(|value| value.to_str().unwrap().to_owned()))
7469        };
7470
7471        let scoped = ForwardPolicy::decode("0,detcore::random=1").unwrap();
7472        let mut command = Command::new("/bin/true");
7473        let duplicate = request_liteinst_detlog_forwarding(&mut command, &scoped)
7474            .unwrap()
7475            .expect("a policy that forwards a target requests forwarding");
7476        let passed = env(&command, LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_ENV)
7477            .flatten()
7478            .expect("the descriptor is passed");
7479        assert_eq!(
7480            detcore::detlog::tool_output_env_target(&passed),
7481            Some(duplicate.as_raw_fd()),
7482            "{passed}"
7483        );
7484        let sent = env(&command, LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_POLICY_ENV)
7485            .flatten()
7486            .expect("the policy goes with the descriptor");
7487        let sent = ForwardPolicy::decode(&sent).unwrap();
7488        assert_eq!(sent, scoped);
7489        assert!(sent.forwards("detcore::random"));
7490        assert!(!sent.forwards("detcore::tool_local"));
7491
7492        let nothing = ForwardPolicy::decode("0").unwrap();
7493        let mut command = Command::new("/bin/true");
7494        command
7495            .env(LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_ENV, "9")
7496            .env(LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_POLICY_ENV, "1");
7497        assert!(
7498            request_liteinst_detlog_forwarding(&mut command, &nothing)
7499                .unwrap()
7500                .is_none()
7501        );
7502        assert_eq!(env(&command, LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_ENV), Some(None));
7503        assert_eq!(
7504            env(&command, LITEINST_DETLOG_FORWARD_POLICY_ENV),
7505            Some(None)
7506        );
7507    }
7508
7509    /// The forwarding policy answers as a real in-process `detlog!` record
7510    /// behaves under the CLI's filter: a record is logged only when both its
7511    /// `tracing::enabled!` hint and its event pass, and a directive with a
7512    /// field filter applies to the hint whatever its fields but to the event
7513    /// only when the event has that field. `info,<module>[{unused}]=warn`
7514    /// suppresses the record; the policy once forwarded it, because it probed
7515    /// only the event.
7516    #[test]
7517    fn detlog_forward_policy_matches_real_detlog_emission_under_field_filters() {
7518        use std::sync::Arc;
7519        use std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize;
7520        use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
7521
7522        use detcore::detlog::ForwardPolicy;
7523        use tracing::level_filters::LevelFilter;
7524        use tracing_subscriber::layer::SubscriberExt;
7525
7526        use crate::liteinst_bootstrap::EffectiveFilter;
7527
7528        struct Count(Arc<AtomicUsize>);
7529        impl<S: tracing::Subscriber> tracing_subscriber::Layer<S> for Count {
7530            fn on_event(
7531                &self,
7532                _event: &tracing::Event<'_>,
7533                _context: tracing_subscriber::layer::Context<'_, S>,
7534            ) {
7535                self.0.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
7536            }
7537        }
7538
7539        let module = module_path!();
7540        for (raw, level) in [
7541            (format!("info,{module}[{{unused}}]=warn"), LevelFilter::INFO),
7542            (
7543                format!("warn,{module}[{{unused}}]=trace"),
7544                LevelFilter::WARN,
7545            ),
7546            (
7547                format!("info,{module}[{{message}}]=warn"),
7548                LevelFilter::INFO,
7549            ),
7550            (format!("info,{module}=warn"), LevelFilter::INFO),
7551            (format!("warn,{module}=info"), LevelFilter::WARN),
7552            ("info".to_owned(), LevelFilter::INFO),
7553            ("warn".to_owned(), LevelFilter::WARN),
7554        ] {
7555            let emitted = Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(0));
7556            let subscriber = tracing_subscriber::registry()
7557                .with(EffectiveFilter::from_directives_lossy(&raw, level).into_filter())
7558                .with(Count(emitted.clone()));
7559            let forwards = tracing::subscriber::with_default(subscriber, || {
7560                detcore::detlog!("probe record");
7561                ForwardPolicy::from_current_subscriber(&raw).forwards(module)
7562            });
7563            assert_eq!(
7564                forwards,
7565                emitted.load(Ordering::Relaxed) == 1,
7566                "RUST_LOG={raw:?} --log={level}: the policy and a real detlog! disagree"
7567            );
7568        }
7569    }
7570}
